PCW — Pay-per-view

Shanghaied 2003, 26 January 2003

26 January 2003
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Shanghaied 2003 26 Jan 2003 The Shanghai Gymnasium, Shanghai, China Red Right Hand As the PCW Shanghaied logo fades out, the Black screen is bathed in flames that accompany "Red Right Hand" by Nick Cave and the Bad Seeds. Take a little walk to the edge of town, Go across the tracks, Where the viaduct looms, like a bird of doom, As it shifts and cracks... Through the fires and smoke we see the twisted and sadistic grinning face of PCW’s World Champion... The Demon known as Clyde. The glistening Gold of the PCW World Title reflects in his eyes. Where secrets lie in the border fires, in the humming wires, Hey man, you know, you're never coming back, Past the square, past the bridge, past the mills, past the stacks... The image of the belt fades from his right eye to be replaced with the image of his satanic ally, DarkStar. On a gathering storm comes a tall handsome man, In a dusty black coat with... A RED RIGHT HAND The flames lick up over Clyde’s face to show him face to face with Snow... His evil smirk matched by a determined growl from his challenger. You'll see him in your nightmares, you'll see him in your dreams, He'll appear out of nowhere but he ain't what he seems, You'll see him in your head, on the TV screen, And hey buddy, I'm warning you to turn it off... A Cage mesh slams across their images, dividing their form up into small diamonds as blood begins to trickle down, tinting both men red. You're one microscopic cog in his catastrophic plan, Designed and directed by... HIS RED RIGHT HAND The blood-hued figures of Snow and Clyde then fade out into the Shanghaied logo before, with the sound of deafening pyrotechnics going off, we cut to the interior of the Shanghai Gymnasium where Red explosions go off from the ring, ramp and the JumboTron. The camera does a pan of the jam-packed Gymnasium where the fans all jostle to get themselves and their signs on camera, Welcome to PCW Shanghaied 2003 "HELLO AND WELCOME TO PCW SHANGHAIED 2003!!!" The yelling voice of Dave Kern cuts through the wildly cheering crowd as the camera finds the announce desk at ringside where the trio of Dave Kern, Jeff Marx and Eleanor Kannon-Hall are seated. Dave: I’m Dave Kern, here with Jeff Marx and Ellie~!...We are LIVE from the Shanghai Gymnasium, scene of probably the most memorable moment in PCW and every spare inch of the Gym has been filled by these PCW-hungry fans! Jeff: Ah, Hubbard bless lax fire-safety! Eleanor: Whooo! Like, YAY PPVs!!! Dave: We have another HUGE PCW and RUSH PPV card here tonight, every PCW Title minus the Tag are up for grabs, as well all the RUSH Titles, and while we have no Tag Team Titles match, the two Champions are facing each other in a First Blood Match! Jeff: DA JESTER! has it wrapped up, just like Xavier, Brandi and Tim! Eleanor: YAY THEM!!! Dave: All the Front Office are in action as Xavier takes on Duke Williams, looking to avenge the loss he suffered at Decimation. Brandi faces Teresa Tomas after months of tormenting her, and will look to regain the TV Title while Tim Walden will look to have the Cruiserweight Title around his waist once again as he faces El Romantico for the belt their last match vacated. Jeff: All that and..... DA MAN!!! Dave: That’s right, in our Main Event we’ll see the first EVER Hell in the Hell in the Cell Match! Clyde will defend his PCW World Title against Snow in what could well go down as the most brutal, bloody and downright lethal match in PCW history if what I’ve heard about the set-up is right! Jeff: Blah, blah, blah... Time to have a Match! Eleanor: YAY MATCHES!!! Mani Shigeru vs Shin Yagyuu LOSER LEAVES TOWN MATCH MANI SHIGERU Vs. SHIN YAGYUU Vs. Clay: THE FOLLOWING IS A SPECIAL LOSER LEAVES TOWN MATCH, AND IS SCHEDULED FOR ONE FALL!!!!! "Self Deception" by Laguna Coil begins. Clay: FIRST MAKING HIS WAY TO THE.... Clayborne stops short and stares at the ramp as Shin Yagyuu prances out carrying a very familiar paper bag. Shin: Taco Bell, Taco Bell, product placement for Taco Bell. Clay: .....RING. Suddenly a gong rings out....music begins as Mani Shigeru leaps out of nowhere, kicking Shin in the back of the head!!!!! Shin tumbles forward, straight through the air before hitting the ringpost and slumping to the ground. Mani Shigeru wastes no time in flying again, smacking Shin in the forehead with a big kick just as he stands!!!!! Yagyuu shakes his head, then squeals. Shin: I see you enjoy my "face to your fist" technique! Yagyuu attempts a punch, but Mani merely sidesteps and trips him, sending him flying clear across the ramp!!!!! Shin: NO FAIR!!!!!! Mani looks on, then follows Shin as he rolls and rolls and rolls straight up the ramp. Shin: Ah, let's see how you like the "your foot to my balls" technique! Shigeru shrugs, then winds up and punts Shin straight in the crotch!!!!!! Crowd: Ooooooooooooooooooooooh! Yagyuu squeals again, then doubles over in agony and hops away behind the curtains. Dave: ...... Mani shakes his head sadly, then gives chase only to get a glass container broken over his head!!!!! Shin walks back out, making satisfied and evil noises. Shin: (Sneering) Yeheeeeeeeeeeh. Now I have you, yeheeeeeeeeeeeh. Jeff: ...... Shin Yagyuu grabs his rival by the ankles and begins leading him through the entryway. As soon as he gets out in the hall though Mani kicks him, causing him to fly straight through the wall!!!!! Shin: (Muffled) Ow. Shigeru gets to his feet and dusts himself off, then climbs through the hole his foe made with his head. He walks up to Yagyuu, who rests on his face in a very painful looking position. Shin: Little help. Mani offers his assistance by punting Shin as hard as he can in the ribs!!!!! He then rolls him over, as referee JJ trots over. JJ: One.....two......WHAT COMES AFTER TWO!?!? Crowd: Thr..... NO!!!!! Shin kicks out!!!!!! Jeff: DAMMIT!!!!!! Ellie: Yes. Mani pulls Yagyuu up and leads him out of the parking lot through a nearby door. He then blinks and throws a hand up as light assaults his eyes. Dave: .....what the hell? Shigeru slowly blinks, looking upon a vast green valley. Mani is abruptly speared to the ground by Shin Yagyuu!!!! Shin smacks his opponent several times, then snaps his fingers. In a flash of light a railroad track materializes through the plain. Ellie: ..... Shin hefts Mani up, then whips out a rope and hogties him to the tracks!!!!! He then stands over him, tapping his fingers and cackling in a Snidely Whiplash-like manner. Shin: Mwahahahahaha! A "choo choo" sound rings out, as the lights of an oncoming train speed towards the helpless Mani!!!!! Mani: (Girlish voice-over) Help help, save me! An all too familiar tune begins filtering over the horizon. Shin: (Turning) Blast, not that accursed Dudley Do Right! Indeed, a man in a mountie outfit trots over the hills on a horse while riding backwards. It reaches the pair.....Yagyuu looks on in confusion. Shin: .....you're not Dudley Do Right. Ike Turner: What the problem is! Ike hops off his horse....which then stands, revealing itself as not a horse but in fact a very downtrodden looking Tina Turner. Tina Turner: Ma.....ma. Ike Turner: Bitch! Who told you could talk? Ike Turner bitchslaps his wife, then pulls her up by the feet. Shin Yagyuu shakes his head, as meanwhile the speeding train roars closer. Mani: (Voice over) Er, can someone assist..... He looks up, the reflection of the huge train close in his eyes. Mani: (Voice over).....me. AGGGGGGH!!!!! The train then rumbles straight up over the top of Mani, crushing him flat!!!!! Crowd: PCW! PCW! PCW! PCW! PCW! PCW! PCW! PCW! PCW! PCW! Shin looks down upon his crushed rival, laughing. Shin: Ahahaha.....ahaha.....ahahaAGH!!!!!! The evil one then falls straight down as Ike Turner literally swings Tina like a rag doll, bashing him upside the head with her!!!!!! Ike: (Swinging) What the problem is! Again, again Ike beats Shin with Tina's limp body!!!!! Finally he ceases, sniffing the air and dropping her. Ike: I smells crack I does! Pure, uncut kitty cat! Ike races off into the distance, as Shin crawls on his hands and knees. Shin: When....will....the.....hurting.....stop..... Shaking off the cobwebs, Yagyuu glances in the direction of the fallen Mani. He starts to crawl towards him.....but a huge shadow falls over him. Shin: Oh crap. From out of nowhere leaps a huge cow!!!!! It strikes a kung fu pose, growing and mooing. Shin: (Sighing) Oh fuck it, just kill me already. The cow scraps poor Shin up in her hooves, then begins beating him savagely!!!!!! Jeff: ...... Shin Yagyuu flies about as the bovine warrior thrashes him, literally throwing him about!!!!!! He tries to escape, but the cow simply leaps into the air and CRUSHES HIM WITH ALL HER WEIGHT!!!!!! Cow: Moooooooooo! The cow rubs it's hind quarters down onto Shin's body, adding injury to insult to injury. It then stands, dusting herself off and cartwheeling away as Mani Shigeru walks up nonchalantely. Jeff: ......okay time out. I can accept Ike Turner, Tina Turner, the train and the cow. But WHY IS MANI STILL ALIVE!? Dave: Death doesn't really bother him too much. Jeff: Oh well that just explains SO MUCH. Mani shakes his head, then puts a foot on Shin's chest. JJ materializes in a flash of light. JJ: One.....two......WHAT COMES AFTER TWO!?!?! Crowd: THREE!!!!!! Clay: THE WINNER OF THE MATCH......MANI SHIGERU!!!!!!! Dave: And let us NEVER.... EVER... Talk of this match again? Jeff: Agreed. Eleanor: Like, SO agreed. Dave: And that goes for you readers too. (NEW to PCWShopZone.com : Kano’s ROCKS~! - Geology brought to you by PCW’s REAL Legend.) Jackson Rust's entourage barges in SEE-THROUGH LINGERIE AND A CRYSTALLIZED LAPTOP The scene cuts back to Jackson Rust's locker room, where he is sat down upon a chair, adjusting his boots as his entourage flocks the room, barging through the door. Rust looks up at them quizzically. Jackson Rust: And you have the nerve to wonder why I don't invite you to most important shows? Kimberly: Sorry, Al decided to stalk Ellie~! for a bit. Al: Yeah, sorry....but she's so Preppilicious. Steve: P-per-pervert. Jackson sighs, and reaches behind him into his bag, and pulls out the 2D rodent, Lemmiwinks. Jackson Rust: You see why humans are so inferior to gerbils? Lemmiwinks nods. Lemmiwinks: *squeaks* Al: C'mon, that's insulting that you'd rather invite a gerbil you came into possession of a week ago, then your friends that you've known for years. Jackson shrugs. Jackson Rust: So, what's your point? Steve: O-ouch...v-ver-very o-ouch. Kimberly: Steve, has anyone told you not to impersonate Austin Powers? Steve: Y-yes... Kimberly: For the love of God, listen to them. Jackson finishes the task of tying his boot, before standing up, lifting Lemmiwinks onto his bare shoulder. Jackson Rust: How about this...you leave me alone until I finish my big Pay Per View match against Storm; and I buy you, Al -- an Ellie~! foam finger. Al: Deal. Jackson Rust: You, Steve -- a "lose your lisp in thirty days" tape. Steve: I d-do-don't ha-have a l-lisp. Jackson Rust: Oh, alright then. Then I'll buy you a "disguise your stutter in twenty days" tape. Steve: D-deal. Kimberly: What about me? Jackson Rust: See-through lingerie? Kimberly: No. Jackson Rust: See-through lingerie and a crystallized laptop? Kimberly: You drive a hard bargain, but alright. They all smile toward the unusual generosity of Jackson. Jackson Rust: Now, I mean this in all due respect, but stay in this room. Don't make any noise. Don't move. Don't think. Don't do anything until I get back. Al: So much for friends. Jackson Rust: An Ellie~! foam finger with Prep Slap 'O' Doom action. Al: So, let's have a nice sit, eh? Jackson Rust: Very good. Jackson places Lemmiwinks down on the bench he was sat at, before reaching into the bag, and pulling out a plastic rolling ball, putting the Gerbil F'N King in, and letting him roll around before walking out of the locker room door, and pulling out a conveniently placed lock on the door. A change of plans A CHANGE OF PLANS Cutting away to the Front Office suite we see the focused face of PCW CEO, Xavier Kannon as he watches the wrestlers leave ringside on the monitor, pacing back and forth while cracking his knuckles. "Er... Can I get you anything Mr. Kannon, Sir?" Standing behind him, nervously trying to supply his needs is the Front Office’s lackey, the Pimply Faced Intern Kid. Xavier turns to him, not appreciating the interruption. Xavier: Yes, I’ll have one of you to go.... Away. Lowering his head with a whimper, the Intern scurries out the door, nearly missing a collision with PCW Personnel Director Brandi Wilson who is then entering. Brandi: Watch where you’re going, moron. As Pimply apologises about 20 times before running off, Brandi mutters under her breath. Brandi: ... Unless you’re running into busy traffic. Shaking her head and joining Xavier in watching the crowd on the monitors, Brandi surveys what she sees with disgust. Brandi: Can’t we at least set up a few sweatshops while we’re here? At least make this un-sanitary trip worth it. Xavier’s mouth forms a smile, seeing a bit of his own ruthlessness in his Personnel Director. Xavier: Peons... Listen to them cheer, they really have no clue, do they? Like performing Seals they just clap for whatever we throw in front of them, hell, they’ll probably blow the roof off this glorified barn for the Main Event. Miss Wilson’s face turns sour. Brandi: I can’t believe in a company like this, we have the first Pay-Per-View of 2003 headlined by a burned FREAK and an Ex-Con... If the world were more evolved, we’d be a laughing stock. Xavier: Then maybe we should end this show on something the whole World should see and take notice of. I let Duke show me up at Decimation, but tonight I’ll finally show him up as the over-the-hill, washed-up, out of shape Redneck he is.... In the Main Event. A conceited grin crosses his face as Brandi nods. Brandi: Now THAT, is ultimately more palatable... Well, apart from having Duke in it. Xavier: Well, I get to do anything I want to Duke with no-time limit so I wouldn’t expect to see Duke much after tonight, finally time to put that old hoss out to pasture. Both close their eyes and imagine PCW come Monday morning with no Duke. Xavier: Call it an ‘Involuntary Redundancy’ if you will... That or me retiring him for fun. Nodding in approval, Brandi still looks a little concerned. Brandi: What about the RUSH Resistance? You know they’ll latch onto anything to get them publicity. Xavier: I was about to talk to you about that, I have a little plan I’ll need your contract knowledge for. As both the Front Office members look to each other with evil intention in their eyes out shot cuts back to the announcers. Matt Hunt (c) vs Stimpo RUSH NETWORK TITLE MATT HUNT(c) Vs. STIMPO Vs. Dave: Well, we’ve got a Network title bout up next that should rock this place! Jeff: It will rock if mein homie, Matt Hunt, wins. If jobberdom incarnate wins, I think I’ll riot. Clay: THE FOLLOWING CONTEST IS SCHEDULED FOR ONE FALL…AND IS FOR THE RUSH NETWORK CHAMPIONSHIP! Dave: On a night of surprises, I'm sure Matt Hunt has got a few in store for Stimpo. Jeff: When will you learn, moron? It's Matthias Kuntz-Hunt, not Matt Hunt. Dave: Oh yeah…right…sure. Ellie~!: He should change his name to, like, Ellie~! Hunt…that’s WAY better. Monty Python's "The Spanish Inquisition" hits the arena, which reacts in a huge pop from the fans. Moments later, Stimpo appears at the entrance way and with a focused expression makes his way to the ring. Clay: INTRODUCING FIRST…THE CHALLENGER! FROM MEXICO AND WEIGHING IN AT 189LBS…STIMPO!!! The luchadore enters the ring and limbers up as his music slowly fades. Dave: Stimpo looking in great condition. Jeff: Which is all well and good, but Hunt is gonna squash him – he outweighs this "Mexican" by almost 100 lbs! Without warning, the arena is cut into darkness and the fans voice their disapproval at the pending entrance of DDT by booing their heads off. The countdown begins… Zehn…Neun…Acht… Dave: Great, more showboating from this ass… Sieben…Sechs…Funf… Jeff: Shush, you're interupting the countdown! Vier…Drei…Zwei… Jeff: Get ready for das booooooom! AUS!! A collection of fireworks explode at the entranceway which light up the whole arena in an orange glow as Rammestein's "Sonne" erupts into the arena. Ellie~!: Eeekzies! Jeff: I’ll comfort you, my doll. With the house lights back to normal service, Hunt emerges from the entrance way with his five-man strong Bundeswehr marching behind him. Dave: *sarcastically* Well, here comes the fearless champion Jeff: Hey, Kannon offered him the position and he would have been an idiot to turn it down! Ellie~!: Kannon?! That’s like SO my last name! Clay: HIS OPPONENT…FROM HAMBURG, GERMANY AND WEIGHING IN AT 290LBS…THE RUSH NETWORK CHAMPION…"DAS DANGEROUS TECHNIK" MATTHIAS KUNTZ-HUNT!!! Jeff: At least that capitalist pig got it right The fans boo as Hunt orders his crew to stay on the outside as he unstraps his title and holds in one hand as he slowly enters the ring. Dave: Hunt's got a twinkle in his eye…he's up to something. Jeff: That’s called the look of a genius at work. Hunt cautiously steps into the ring, not taking an eye off his foe as "Sonne" fades out and within seconds he uncharacteristically tosses the Network Title belt at Saddle, who misses at the last minute and the strap drops to her feet. Dave: Hunt just tossed that belt as if it was a piece of trash! Stimpo motions to lunge at Hunt, but the German holds his hand up to halt him and demands a mic. Ellie~!: He’s gonna be all, like, "SEIG HAIL" and stuff. Hunt is dually passed a mic and don't hesitate in talking. Matthias Kuntz-Hunt: Stimpo, I've heard a few murmurs on the street. I've heard people say that they don't care about this match, that they'd rather shave their Uncle's hairy back than watch you – a highflying luchadore – and me – one of the best technical wrestlers to have ever come out of Europe – go at it for that Network Title. Dave: I didn't hear that, what's the source? Jeff: Oh yeah, that’s the word on the streets. I’ve heard it from all the smarks. Stimpo glances at Hunt and Saddle with a bewildered expression. Matthias Kuntz-Hunt: It's true and I've also heard that people don't rate me as the Network Champion and they probably never will. Jeff: How dare those commoners! Matthias Kuntz-Hunt: So, going on this information and the fact that you like to please the people…lets kill two birds with one stone. Dave: Is this true? It wouldn't surprise me to be honest. A suspicious expression grows on Stimpo as Hunt continues. Matthias Kuntz-Hunt: In about 30 seconds, I will lay down on my back in the center of this ring…all you have to do is cover me and the match and the RUSH Network Title is all yours… There's a reaction of shock from the fans as Hunt shrugs and holds his arms out, allowing the mic to drop and land on the canvas creating a thud. Stimpo shakes his head in disbelief as Hunt slowly starts to lay on the mat. Dave: He's really going through with it! Ellie~!: Erm…won’t he lose the match and stuff? Jeff: What in the great name of Adolf Hitler is he doing? Hunt is now staring up at the arena lights as Stimpo glances around. The roar of the fans urges the "Mexican" to inch closer and closer and he duely obliges. Dave: This could be the shortest Network Title Match ever!! Jeff: Umm….Kuntz…Hunt…whatever your new name is….please please please get up! A cautious Stimpo drops to his knees and slowly hooks the leg, all the while being weary of any tricks that Hunt may pull. Ellie~!: Like, BORING! Referee Sally drops to her knees as the excitement rises. However, she pulls back after motioning to hit the mat the first time and pleads that this is ludicrous. Still on his back, Hunt shouts for her to count…but she hesitates! Dave: This is surreal! Now Saddle's hesitating! Moments later, she returns to vertical base and shakes her head insisting that both men get back to their feet too. The fans voice their disapproval and the booing is thunderous. Dave: Don’t’ have a conscience check NOW! Count the damn pin! Stimpo rolls off Hunt as the German himself slowly gets back to his feet. Saddle calls both men to her as she speaks, but due the noise it's impossible to hear what she is saying. Dave: Looks like Sally wants a decent match and I can't blame her! Jeff: Damn, Hunt fooled all of you stupid Jews. Dave sighs Hunt and Stimpo both back away nodding their heads, the German reluctantly agreeing to whatever the ref sa Dave: Despite Sally's second thoughts, you have to question Hunt's sanity here… Ellie~!: It’s not over yet? As soon as Saddle signals for the bell, Hunt lands a clothesline to the unsuspecting Stimpo who falls to the mat instantly. He then starts to viciously stomp the luchdore, who bounces off the mat with each clomp. Dave: Woah, what a shot! Hunt isn't gonna get his way so he's gonna be Mr Nasty again. Jeff: For a moment, I thought we had lost DDT…but he's back!! He then aggressively pulls his up by the neck and executes a harsh Dortmunder – a T-Bone suplex where Stimpo flies across the ring. Hunt jumps back to his feet and pulls Stimpo up again, this time locking his giant arms around the Mexican and hits Die Berliner – a Bearhug into a Belly-to-Belly suplex with effect! Dave: Good grief, Hunt is going postal here! Ellie~!: Hee hee, he should WAY wear one of those cool postman outfits. They’re WAY cute! Jeff: Brilliant, my queen. The fast-paced start continues as Hunt immediately goes for the pin and hooks the leg. Saddle slides into action… One… Two… Kickout! The fans cheer as Stimpo kicks out, but Hunt is relentless as he pulls a limp Stimpo up and whips him into the ropes. On the return, he hooks his foe up and hits Die Munchener – a harrowing spinning Spinebuster! Dave: Woah, Hunt really is pulling out the stops tonight and nobody here can believe it! Jeff: *with bad German accent* Die Meanstreaken Jr.! Hunt pulls Stimpo to his feet again and uses his power to pick him up and military press him before hurling him to the outside and at the feet of Jurgen and Otto – two members of the Bundeswehr. Dave: Uh-oh…danger for Stimpo. Jeff: As if he'd know what's going on…he's totally out of it! As Hunt distracts Saddle inside the ring, Jurgen and Otto put the boots to Stimpo before rolling him back into the ring. DDT instantly pulls him to the center of the ring and covers him again… One… Two… Kickout!! Ellie~!: Otto…? He sounds, like, WAY sexy! Jeff: You have NO idea! Dave: You’re both strange. Jeff: *smirks* Hunt doesn't seem frustrated at Stimpo as he continues on this legal assault. He pulls him towards the turnbuckle, before mounting them himself. Dave: Where is he going? Hunt isn't a high flyer! Jeff: He’s got a plan, don’t you worry. Ellie~!: I hope he does a mega senton shooting star 5-star moonsault slam…..and stuff! Surprisingly, Hunt reaches the top in no time and stands on the turnbuckle before leaping off with a shooting star press!!!! However, Stimpo manages to roll away at the last minute and Hunt hits the mat face first! Dave: Good grief, the whole ring shook!! Jeff: A 290 pound man executing a shooting star press!! I don't believe it! Praise be to Hitler for giving us the gift of DDT! Dave: Hunt's been derailed here and it could be the opening that Stimpo has been waiting for!! Instead of an instant attack, Stimpo himself slowly climbs the turnbuckle as Hunt is also getting back to his feet. DDT staggers around as Stimpo awaits, perched on the top turnbuckle. As soon as Hunt turns face-to-face, the Mexican leaps off and lands a stunning dropkick right between the eyes!! Dave: Missile dropkick!! Stimpo's back in this!! Ellie~!: Hee hee, all those big bald guys on the outside are, like, way going crazy. Das Bundeswehr are going nuts on the outside as Stimpo scrambles across and hooks the leg… One… Two… Kickout!! Dave: Oh my god, I thought that was it! Hunt just got the shoulder up! Jeff: HEIL HUNT!!! HEIL HUNT!!! With Hunt still dazed, Stimpo jumps to the turnbuckle again. However, Karl – one of Das Bundeswehr – leaps up onto the apron, but he loses his footing and slips off! Hunt slowly returns to physical base and, like before, staggers around to face the perched Stimpo. Seconds later, the luchdore flies off and hits a Hurracarana!!! The people roar as Stimpo hooks both legs while still on top of Hunt!! One… Two… Thr…Kickout!!!! Dave: Another close call!!! Hunt is losing his grip on his title! Ellie~!: Xavi always tells me I need to get a better grip when I’m giving- Jeff: Wait a second, lemme turn on the recorder so I can have this for when I jer- Dave: MATCH MATCH MATCH NOW! The second Hunt somehow kicks out of the cover, Stefan – another Bundeswehr member – leaps onto the apron! Stimpo spots the incoming German and sprints towards him, landing a dropkick, which sends the bulky Stefan tumbling back to the outside! The crowd warn Stimpo as Wilheim attempts to enter the ring on the other side and again, the Mexican flies at him to land a forearm to send him back down as well!!! Dave: Stimpo's fighting back here and he knows that has to do that to even come close to winning the title tonight!! Jeff: Nobody can keep this up…especially no spic wannabe jobber. No sooner as Raves spoke, when Stimpo turns around to receive a hammering big boot right between the eyes. The Mexican hits the mat faster than a sack of potatoes as Hunt composes himself. Jeff: HA! DIE! Dave: *sighing* Hunt back in control here. With Stimpo in the center of the ring, Hunt staggers towards the turnbuckle and starts to climb…again. The fans rise, in anticipation of what will happen next and hoping that Stimpo will reverse this momentum. Jeff: I've never seen Hunt this possessed before!! Dave: He's going upstairs again! Hunt is finally at the top and before deciding what to do, he taunts the fans by holding his arms out and shrugging. The booing is relentless! Ellie~!: Mega senton corkscrew swanton leg drop bomb thingy, like, version two! Hunt dives off the turnbuckle, all the way across to the center of the ring and hits his intended target with a deafening diving headbutt – Die Reeperbahn!!!! Even the fans can't believe what they've just seen!! Dave: Oh my god!! Hunt flew across the ring like a bird and hit Stimpo like a damn hammer!! Ellie~!: I, like, believe I can fly and stuff… Jeff: WOOOOOO!!!!!! Game Over!! Hunt obviously felt the effects of the move and remains on the canvas himself. However, he shakes the cobwebs and lays an arm across the motionless Stimpo's chest… One… Two… Thr…Kickout!!! Dave: He kicked out!! Stimpo kicked out!! Ellie~!: *filing her nails* Can this thing, like, end? I’m way bored down here. Hunt stumbles to his feet using the ropes and leans down to pull up his foe. Suddenly, Stimpo lands a punch to the stomach, which shocks Hunt! The German reaches down again, but the luchadore hits another right and another and another!! Stimpo is now back to his feet, taps Hunt in the mid section and hits the Latino Layout!!! A stunning DDT!!! The roof blows off the place!! Dave: Latino Layout!! It's got to be over!! Ellie~!: DDT on…err….DDT! Yay DDT! Jeff: Idiot…errr…you’re ravishing as always. Stimpo immediately goes for the pin… One… Two… Thr…kickout!! Stimpo rolls off Hunt holding his head in disbelief and questions the count made by Saddle. The fans themselves groan in disappointment. Dave: So close again! Hunt isn't going down without a fight! Jeff: Hunt isn’t losing either. Stimpo fights the frustration as Hunt starts to stir, shaking the cobwebs. Without warning, Stimpo hits the ropes and on the return hits a cross body, which takes down Hunt upon impact! Dave: Stimpo's on a roll here, he might actually do it! Jeff: Not if DDT has anything to do with it! Ellie~!: Wait….aren’t these guys in RUSH?! Why are they, all, like on my show?! Dave sighs Stimpo leaps off Hunt and charges towards the turnbuckle, mounting it upon impact. The fans rise to their feet as he waits a few seconds, leaps off and executes the stunning 540 Stimpo Splash!!!! A thunderous roar arises from the people!! Dave: Stimpo Splash!! Surely, we've got a new Network Champion!! Jeff: No!!!! Stimpo has the leg hooked upon impact and Sally slides into position… One… Two… Thr… Dave: What the hell?!? Otto breaks the count by pulling Saddle underneath the bottom rope and to the outside!! Ellie~!: Like, he just exudes sexiness. Dave: Now that you mention it, all people with the name Otto are MAD sexy. As the arena changes to thunderous booing, Sally slaps Otto right across the cheek before landing a swift kick to the crown jewels!!! Dave: Woah, Sally taking action with effect!!! Jeff: She can't do that!!! As the ref slides back into the ring, Stimpo leaps in and hits a baseball slide which connects with Otto's kidneys. The German goes down as Stefan hops on to the apron again, but Stimpo sprints across the ring and hits an elbow to his chest and sending him down!! Wilheim then attempts to enter the ring, but Stimpo hits a low blow on him before pushing him out of the ring. In a cold, damp basement IN A COLD, DAMP BASEMENT Voice: Let her empower me, she which watches over us… A man in a long, black trench coat is seen kneeling before a strange, intimidating alter omitting a glowing blue aura. Man: Ok, I paid homage to you. Now what? And then, something happens you’d think would only happen on a bad episode of the Twilight Zone: The statue speaks. Statue: Excellent, Lunis Superia. I shall act as your guide and source of power. Man: (Sighs) I don’t know about this… Statue: If you have faith in the True Way, you will follow through with your task, Lunis Superia. The man, known as ‘Lunis Superia’, merely stares at the statue for a moment with a look of puzzlement and pondering on his face. He runs a gloved hand through his jet-black hair and nods, realizing what he must do. Man: Alright… as you wish, my Goddess. Statue: And just to make sure you go about this correctly, I shall also accompany you. Man: What?? And before the strange figure can react, the eyes of the statue light up in a blaze of angry flames. A VOICE FROM THE WILDERNESS The scene opens on Skylar Green sitting in his locker room, lacing up his boots in readying for the Pay-Per-View match-up tonight. A mellow knock echoes from outside, to which Green pauses his preparation. His reply comes without moving his head. Sky: Enter. From off-camera the door opens with a squeak and closes with nary a sound. Green resumes tying his boots as a figure cloaked in black robes approaches and takes the side of the blind superstar, this again generating a pause from Skylar as he senses the presence of the visitor. Sky: What is it you want? I’m a tad busy right now. The visitor drops his hood, revealing the solemn face of a youth no more than 25 years old. His dark hair is shortly cropped and a single bizarre rune is painted forebodingly on his forehead. In one hand he clutches a small black box, which can be discerned by most as a tape recorder. ???: Skylar Green I presume? Green smirks with mock amusement. He tilts his head faintly to the side momentarily before replying. Sky: It’s not obvious, I take it? With my name being stamped on the door and all? ???: Forgive me, sir. I bring a message from my colleague. Well... my master, your colleague, that is. Green sighs indignantly. Sky: Whatever... It's not a letter, is it? ???: No, no. My master is aware of your sight condition. And he thought it would be more enticing to send an aural message to you, instead. Sky: Audio message, genius. ???: ... Whatever. The robed man places the recorder on the bench beside Skylar Green. He presses the PLAY button and a whirring sound emanates from the speakers. Voice: You are truly blessed, Skylar Green. You have the dubious honor of my master’s attention. Green sneers in the direction of the strange youth. Sky: You may leave now. The enigmatic messenger draws his hood back up over his head and bids his leave without another word. Green leans back against a locker with arms crossed ruggedly about his chest as a voice rumbles forth from the recorder. It is masked electronically so that the voice is warped and unidentifiable. An ominous snicker opens up. Voice: Ah, The End.. or should I refer to you now as Skylar Green? Ah well, I suppose I’ll humor you even though I much prefer the former moniker. Now, I’m sure you’re wondering who in the holy name of Christ has the lack of sense to send an errand boy to your locker room before one of the biggest matches you’ve had in this God-forsaken company. Sky: Not particularly, no. Leaving the tape playing, Skylar leaves the locker room. As the door slams behind him, the tape keeps playing. Voice: Well, frankly, neither God nor Christ have anything to do with why I’m here, and they certainly have nothing to do with what I have become. Voice: You see, my ignorant little friend, I have evolved significantly since the last time we spoke. The last you saw of me, I was next to nothing. Broken-down, confused, without ambition or guidance. Since then I have been opened to vast annals of knowledge and the sort of secretive realities that men like you fear the very thought of. Now, I’m sure you’re rather baffled as to this entire predicament. Why exactly would I decide out of the blue to approach you like this? Well, the answer is both simple and complex... A sigh is heard over the tape, sounding slightly distant, as though this were just a recitation and not the speaker's words. Voice: ... because frankly, I don’t even know why. I don’t question, Skylar. My mentors issue their orders to me and I fulfill my obligations to them. I am their vessel and their wishes are my commands. It’s that simple. A male hand reaches down and picks up the tape recorder. Bringing it up to his ear, Cal Seaver's face is shown on camera. Voice: I know only one thing, and that one thing alone was enough to draw my presence to you tonight. You have been targeted, Skylar. My masters have decreed you be taken out of the big picture in PCW and so my mission is clear. Whether you win the top contender spot tonight is wholly inconsequential to my objective. You are my quarry and you shall be taken out, regardless. Best of luck tonight, Skylar. And beware the darkness... The strange voice trails off into a chuckle. Cal Seaver turns off the tape and rewinds it. Cal: I gotta get the sound techs to fix up the voice and see who this is. Room becomes a furnace ROOM BECOMES A FURNACE Alan Ackart gazes into television monitor, taking in the previous happenings of Shanghaied 3. Sipping coffee from a Styrofoam cup he turns, heading back to toward the locker room area. Pausing, his face shows his confusion as he scans the various corridors. Uncertainty takes over him as he searches for help, eventually finding it in the form of a backstage assistant. Ackart: E-Excuse me. Assistant: Yes? Confused on how to actually explain his problem without sounding stupid, Ackart tries his best. Ackart: Well, I'm Alan Ackart... and I um... Assistant: Nice to meet you Mr. Ackart, what can I do for you? Staring blankly at the assistant, Alan continues to search for the right words. While his crystal blue eyes scanned the area, he saw a sign. Ackart: Uh, nevermind. I just remembered. The assistant eyes him suspiciously as he walks toward the sign reading "locker rooms". While walking through the dimly lit corridor, the stench of smoke invades his nostrils. He walks toward the sign reading "locker rooms". As Alan nears his locker room, a few security personnel approach him. Guard: Mr. Ackart, you can't go in there. Ackart: Huh? Guard 2: There’s a fire, and it’s in your locker room. Ackart: WHAT??! Thinking about his make up and synthetic materials Alan runs past the two guards. A few seconds later, he forces his way through a mini barricade of wrestlers and other security personnel. As the fireman battles the small flames with an extinguisher, Alan watches in horror as a few of his bags burned. "HOWY SHIT! HOWY SHIT! HOWY SHIT! HOWY SHIT! HOWY SHIT! HOWY SHIT!" The sound of the announcers scrambling for their gear is heard as Ellie~! rummages through the wreckage and holds Xavier in her arms, the Scientologist motionless as Duke collapses inside the ring, trying to get his breath back after the exertion. Dave: XAVIER KANNON WAS JUST LAUNCHED LIKE A MISSILE THROUGH OUR ANNOUNCE DESK!!! Jeff: IF HE’S HURT MY XAVI I’LL KILL HIM!!! Dave: "Your"? Jeff: ELLIE~! CAN NEVER LOVE HIM LIKE I DO! His wife still unable to revive him, Kannon doesn’t move amidst the wood as Duke rolls out beside the announce desk and looks down at Kannon, punching his palm. "DUKE! DUKE! DUKE! DUKE! DUKE!" Cutting backstage we see the RUSH sell-out’s locker room where Chris Matthews is about to rush out and help but is being held back by Tyler Valentine, not wanting to instigate anything at ringside. Dave: In neutering the RUSH Resistance, Kannon has rendered his sell-outs useless too... They’re not going to want to interfere knowing their livelihoods are on the line if it backfires on Kannon. Ellie springs up and starts jawing at Duke, who just stands there and strokes his beard, frustrated with his lack of response, Ellie~! thrusts her right hand back and with all the power vested to her...HITS DUKE WITH THE PREP SLAP O’DOOM~! Dave: I DON’T think that is going to work! Jeff: Er.... Shouldn’t Duke be dead now, like Bobby? Dave: No... Hope.... In... GAP! Just smiling back at her Duke grabs the little prep and turns her around, bending her over to simulate Malice Style to the delight of he crowd. Jeff: I’LL KILL HIM!!! Eleanor: Er... HELLO?!?! This is SO not Ascension! His eyes only just opening, Xavier sees what his wife is enduring at the hands of Duke and feels the adrenaline surge through his system, grabbing a piece of table debris and using every bit of energy left in his body to fly up AND BREAK THE PIECE OF DESK OVER DUKE’S HEAD!!! "OOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOWWWWAAAAAHHHHHHHHH!!!!!!" Duke’s face goes blank and he drops down to the mats, Ellie rushing over to her husband to check on him, Kannon saying he’s okay as he stumbles to the ring apron to support himself on. Dave: It looked like Kannon was down and out, but if there’s own thing on the planet you could say that Kannon cares about, it’s Ellie~! Jeff: Duke just signed his own death certificate wen he touched her. Pulling Duke up, Kannon pushes him back into the ring, needing 3 attempts at it before the President is finally rolled into the ring. Staying at ringside to support her husband, Ellie~! cheers Xavier on as he rolls into the ring after Duke, his back speckled with cuts from the table. "Like...... GO Xavi!!!" Bashing her little hands on the canvas, Ellie plays cheerleader as Kannon stumbles to his feet but immediately has to grab onto the ropes for support. The sounds of anticipation from the crowd, wondering what their eyes will se next is replaced by a jeer of disgust as GAZ GREEN, BRANDI AND TIM WALDEN COME RUNNING DOWN THE AISLE!!! Dave: Oh God, what are they doing here? Jeff: Sweet Hubbard you’re an idiot. Kannon CAN NOT be disqualified... He’s finally got his change to not only beat Duke. but to run that over-the-hill disgrace out PCW and into traction! "BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!!" The camera cuts back to the RUSH Resistance locker room where Matt Elia is being restrained by the Masked Mullet, telling him they can’t do anything as Brandi, Gaz and Walden spread out around ringside, joining with Ellie so a Front Office member is stationed on each side of the ring. Dave: They have the ring surrounded.... WHAT NOW?!?! From the back walk a uniform line of men, all decked out in PCW Security outfits who form a human barrier between the curtain and the aisle. Dave: He has Duke boxed in and cut-off! Jeff: You know what, Kern? I have one of those feelings that this is going to RULE!!! In the ring, Duke is barely moving, failing in an attempt to get on all fours he collapses as Kannon kicks the chair in the ring towards Brandi, it skidding to a halt just before her as she takes it in hand and steps up onto the apron. Behind Kannon, Florien Liebig rolls to his side, slowly coming to. Dave: What is he waiting for? If you’re going to going to do it.... DO IT! Don’t rub it in. Jeff: They’re taking their time to savour the moment... Imagine it, no more Duke in PCW! He’ll be on a Life Support Machine instead. Dave: This is disgusting, and what’s even more disgusting is he’s made it LEGAL! Kannon walks over to Brandi, giving her directions BUT THEY’RE IGNORED AS BRANDI INSTEAD SWINGS THE CHAIR RIGHT ONTO THE TOP OF XAVIER’S HEAD!!! "OOOOOOOOOOOOOOWAHHHHHHHHHHH?!?!?!!?" Dave: WHAT?!?!? BRANDI JUST LEVELLED KANNON!!! OH PLEASE.... LET THIS BE WHAT I THINK IT IS!!! Jeff: WALDEN.... GAZ.... KILL THAT BITCH!!! As Brandi drops down off the apron, Gaz, Walden and Ellie~! don’t move, they just watch Kannon fall back into the middle of the ring. Dave: Brandi has just knocked Kannon the FUCK OUT! And none of the Front Office seem to be doing anything! The crowed are shocked into silence as Florien climbs to his feet, holding his neck and looking down at the fallen Kannon, then to Duke, then to the innocent looking Front Office members at ringside. Amidst the lull in the noise, Duke rolls over and sees Kannon down, the President crawling his way over towards him. Dave: Kannon’s lackeys aren’t helping him.... Not even his own WIFE! Jeff: HAHA! Now she can be mine! Duke crawls closer and closer to Kannon, before reaching out an arm to cover his chest as Florien drops down and makes the count, not a single sound being heard from the fans, the Front Office just watching on as the hand hits the mat Dave: Has the Front Office just made a Hostile Take-over? 1.................. Brandi, Gaz and Tim grin smugly. 2................ The only movement Kannon makes is to breathe. .........3!!!!! "WHOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!!!!" Dave: DUKE WINS! DUKE HAS BEATEN KANNON BY MY EYES STILL DON’T BELIEVE IT AND MY BRAIN DOESN’T UNDERSTAND IT!!! A dazed Duke stumbles up to his feet and has his hand raised by Florien Liebig, who looks suspiciously at the Front Office members around ringside as "Honky Tonk Heroes" hits and Duke’s win gets the rousing applause it deserves. Clay: THE WINNER OF THIS MATCH.......... "THE MALICE MAN" DUKE WILLIAMS!!! Dave: Kannon’s allies just stood their and watches his enemy beat him... WHY? Jeff: I got a better question, where is the NOW single Ellie~! staying? Duke rolls out of the ring, Brandi, still holding the steel chair standing aside as a still groggy and confused Malice Man is helped up the aisle by Florien Liebig. In the ring, Kannon begins to stir as Gaz walks around to Clay and swipes his microphone before rolling into the ring, followed by Brandi, then Walden, then Ellie~! as Duke is helped past Security and through the curtain. Dave: This doesn’t look good.... And after the way a man like Kannon has torn friendships apart in the past, I hope it doesn’t end good either. Jeff: You’re a bitter, bitter man... The 4 of them stand atop Kannon, looking down at him as the Scientologist blinks to combat the bright lights above him before WALDEN REACHES A HAND DOWN WHICH KANNON TAKES! Dave: HUH? Jeff: Man, I REALLY need to be kept up to speed with things! To a gasp of shock and some boos from the Shanghai crowd, Walden gives the CEO a hand up as Gaz is quick to shake his boss’ hand, as is Brandi before he gets a hug and a kiss from Ellie~! Dave: What’s with the happy families? Jeff: FUCK! Does this mean Ellie~! isn’t single anymore? Thunderous boos go our around the Gym as Gaz hands the microphone to Kannon, the Scientologist shaking his head to clear the cobwebs out. He turns to Brandi, rubbing his head. Xavier: You could have gone a little easier on me couldn’t you? Oh well, full marks for putting your all into it. "BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!" Dave: Will someone enlighten me? On the JumboTron, footage of Kannon talking to Marvin Harris earlier is shown, the fans quieting their jeers to hear. Xavier: I’m not having that happen again, especially not in MY Main Event... So there will be no RUSH run-ins, no RUSH interference, I don’t even want to see a RUSH guy not in their locker rooms, comprende? Marvin’s head tilts to the side, questioning the CEO. Marvin: Yeah, right... Just a bit worried that we’ll take out your flunkies around ringside like last time? Because, let’s face it, Xavier... Everytime you’ve met Duke, he’s beaten the living shit out of you... And it’s been kinda fun to watch. Teeth snarling, Xavier responds. Xavier: Oh, there will be NO RUSH members at ringside... Tonight is the night, that in the Main Event I finally retire PCW’s Redneck embarrassment and I’m not risking ANY ‘problem’ arising. You’ve seen with Valentine how fickle you bush-leaguers can be, so if ANY RUSH member, and I mean ANY, costs me this match with Duke you’ll ALL be unemployed come Monday morning, understand? "BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!" Dave: What does that have to do with anything, the RUSH guys stayed in the back like asked..... Unless.... Jeff: Dun Dun DUNNNNNNNNNNNN!!! Xavier: Say, Brandi, I believe you have something to show us... Another hush goes out as from her pocket, Brandi pulls out a roll of paper and slowly unravels it, holding it to the camera to show a RUSH Contract, displayed to all on the Tron with Brandi’s signature on. Dave: Oh God.... He hasn’t.... Kannon looks at the Tron, confused. Xavier: But wait... Wouldn’t you need to clear it with the Personnel Director... Oh, that’s right, it’s YOU!!! Dave: Has he just done what I think he has.... Jeff: You mean you know what the hell is going on?!?!? Suddenly, Gaz slaps his hands to his cheeks, looking agasp as Xavier holds the microphone to him. Gaz: But Squire, doesn’t that mean that all of RUSH are now fired? Realising what Kannon and the Front Office has just done, the Chinese fans bombard the ring with abuse and litter, the 5 in the ring not letting it intrude on their moment. Eleanor: Are......... Now......... Fired! Dave: He has....... He actually has..... WHY?!?! Cutting backstage we see the locker room door of the RUSH Resistance fly open only for the members to be met by a wall of Security, stopping any of them getting through and restraining the wrestlers. The shot switches to the RUSH sell-out’s door where banging is heard from the inside of a locked door as a line of Security also forms outside in case it’s broken down. Dave: WHY?!??! "THEES SUCKS! THEES SUCKS! THEES SUCKS! THEES SUCKS! THEES SUCKS!" Smirking, Kannon swipes Brandi’s contract an rips it up before her eyes. Xavier: I’m sorry, Miss Wilson. This contract is now void.... Good job you have your lucrative PCW deal to fall back on! The insults and jeering continues, before Kannon finally looks out to the crowd, Ellie~! wrapped around him and his Front Office standing beside him, nodding in smugness. Xavier: I suppose you’re wondering WHY? Why get rid of Allies, why get rid of the people who helped me win Alliances? Surely, surely you’d have worked it out by now? Jeff: I HAVE!!! Dave: And? Jeff: I dunno. Xavier: You’re right.... The very men I’m having ejected from the building as we speak did help me win Alliances, the men who Security are manhandling right now are some of the people I’ve fought alongside... But some of them are those who’ve tried to thwart me ever since I saved their company. Dave: He did all this just to get them?!?! The Front Office all nod in agreement, supporting their leader. Xavier: I tried Zero Tolerance, I tried being nice... Okay, I didn’t try being nice, but I got what I wanted from them. I got my Alliances win, I got their TV slot, I got their assets, I got their viewers... But now, I don’t need them. Dave: He’s just casting an entire Fed aside! Jeff: I always said they were losers. Dave: Is that a RUSH tie you’re wearing? Xavier: They’ve served their purpose and now they’re no longer required, hence they’re now being shown the door in a less that friendly manner... You must have realised by now that in order to achieve your goals, others have to be sacrificed... It’s the way of the World. "BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!" Xavier: To get to Jeremy Howard I had to destroy Asylum, to finally wrestle power from Tyler Nelson the Ultimate Authority was sacrificed, Brandi’s friendship with Rent-a-Hero was scrapped to form the Front Office.... And now, to get rid of the RUSH Resistance I’ve destroyed the Fed they were fighting for... Smart business, human nature, and more importantly.... IT WORKS!!! Dave: This is NOT RIGHT! I wasn’t happy when RUSH was bought, but this is peoples livelihoods we’re talking about! Jeff: BAH~! Stop being such a spoilsport! The CEO looks to each of the Front Office members around him in turn, pleased with what he sees. Xavier: I decided it was time to remove the complications... Over the past weeks my attention has wandered. I’ve had the RUSH Resistance as a thorn in my side, I’ve had Duke’s little boycott plunging my profits... But the Resistance are GONE, pretty soon PCW will be SOLD! There will be nothing left to distract me from my goal, regaining the PCW WORLD TITLE! And with the Front Office behind me, my CEO position guaranteed.... WHO IS GOING TO STOP ME?!?!?! His questioning words are met by deafening whistles by the Shanghai crowd before every light in the Gymnasium goes off, not even the JumboTron casting any light. Dave: What the hell? Jeff: I blame Duke, he forgot to pay the bills! Suddenly a beam of light comes from the ceiling and shines on the canvas, a lone letter depicted in it’s illumination. N "OOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOWAHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!!!!" The Front Office look down at the letter cast in the ring by the light, all darting their gazes into the blackness wondering what is going on as another beam emits from the lighting rig high above. V Jeff: Ohhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhh no! No! No! No! No! No! No! No! Dave: I don’t think Xavier will want the lights to come on if this is who I think it is... Jeff: Run.. Just.... RUN!!! Looking around in panic and confusion, the Front Office are like the proverbial rabbit in the headlights as a 3rd column of light lands on the ring canvas. G Dave: N.... V.... G!!! There’s only one of them and he has business with that man in the ring!!! The fans higher up in the Gymnasium read what is spelled out in the ring and pop uncontrollably as in the ring, illuminated by the NVG’s name, the Front Office start to panic... Panicking that is fully justified as "I Stand Alone" by Godsmack starts up, every member of the crowd up on their feet as Kannon storms over to the ropes facing he aisle, gesturing into the darkness for him to come out. Dave: The whole Front Office are in the ring.... Akiyama has left his calling sign.... WHERE IS HE?!?! Suddenly, the columns of light spelling NVG cut off and the arena is again plunged into darkness for a few seconds before with the sound of thunder crashing, in the blink of an eye every light comes back on. ANSWERING FOR HIS ACTIONS Dave: AKIYAMA IS IN THE RING!!! Jeff: WHERE IS SECURITY?!?! The line of Security who were posted by the curtain are laying sprawled out on the stage as slowly, the Front Office turn to be faced with the solemn stare of Akiyama, his right arm slowly rising and pointing to Xavier who’s eyes do nothing to mask his fear. Dave: Akiyama has negated Kannon’s security and the rest are busy containing the RUSH guys backstage! Jeff: RUN!!! Akiyama’s finger stays pointed at Kannon who nervously, as if in a trance raises a finger to his chest and asks ‘Me?’ only for the Neo Void God to nod. Dave: Akiyama is back and he knows who he wants revenge on! The corruption of Kannon split his family, and there’s only one kind of retribution Akiyama knows... DESTRUCTION!!! Jeff: Please Hubbard, make this a swerve... Make this a swerve... Before the raging Japanese God can get to Kannon, Walden breaks off and charges him only for Aki to hop back then spring towards the Financial Reclaimations Executive WITH AN AWESOME YAKUZA KICK RIGHT TO TIM’S JAW THAT SENDS HIM FLIPPING BACKWARDS AND LANDING ON HIS FRONT, OUT COLD! Dave: THE DIVINE SWORD! Akiyama nearly beheaded Tim Walden with that kick and we’re down to four!!! Jeff: Gaz... Please say you have a Tazar or a Cattle Prod! Without hesitation, Gaz "The Jester" Green charges at Akiyama, anger in his Emerald eyes but the Neo Void God calmly watches his approach before, in the blink of an eye, stepping aside to hoist the Entertainment Manager into the air above him, then measuring him FOR A VICIOUS CLOTHESLINE THAT SENDS THE JESTER SPINNING TO THE MAT LIKE A GREEN TORNADO!!!! Dave: DIVINE RETRIBUTION ON THE JESTER! And Retribution will soon be heading Kannon’s way! As soon as Gaz awkwardly lands on the back of his neck, Brandi clenches her fists and rushes Akiyama but the giant takes a huge stride forward AND FLIES INTO HER RIBS WITH THE CELESTIAL SPEAR!!! Dave: The Front Office’s Personnel Director was nearly broken in two by Akiyama, and there’s nobody left to guard Kannon!!! Jeff: This is meant to be HIS moment and that... That.... THING is ruining it! As Walden rolls to the outside and drops to the mats, Kannon paces backwards, holding his hands up to Aki, apologising over and over again but the Neo Void God’s ears are closed to his insincere words and his finger, pointing at Xavier moves across to Ellie~! Dave: He wouldn’t.... Would he? Jeff: NOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!! As Akiyama stalks Ellie~!, Jeff throws his headset off and slides into the ring, hammering away at Akiyama with his fists to no reaction at all before the colour commentator is sent flying into PCW’s preppy Commissioner WITH THE HAND OF FATE!!! Dave: JEFF MARX NEARLY HAD AKIYAMA’S ARM PUNCHED THROUGH HIM! And.... No Jeff or Ellie~!.... This gets better!!! Using Marx’s broken body as cover, Ellie~! makes her escape under the bottom rope and huddling up like a scared rodent, scurries under the ring leaving Xavier with Akiyama and the unconscious bodies of Brandi and Gaz. Dave: Finally, no Front Office, no Security, no Cyclonica, no RUSH.... Just Xavier Kannon and Akiyama!!! The bright Green eyes of Kannon flicker around as Akiyama’s finger once again points at the Scientologist. Growling, Xavier quickly bursts out of the blocks and throws a right hook at Akiyama’s chin but the God simply plucks Kannon’s fist out of mid-air and starts to squeeze it. Dave: Xavier Kannon has done everything to avoid this situation, but it’s all been for nothing as he is at the mercy of Akiyama! "A-KI-YA-MA! A-KI-YA-MA! A-KI-YA-MA! A-KI-YA-MA! A-KI-YA-MA!" Dave: Listen to these fans.... They’re cheering for the NVG and they want him to DESTROY the CEO! Squeezing the fist harder to make a wincing Xavier drop his guard, Akiyama sends a kick into the CEO’s throat that has him gasping for breath before with blurry speed, Aki launches his body up to BRING A SICKENING AXE KICK DOWN ACROSS THE BACK OF XAVIER’S NECK!! Dave: THE GUILLOTINE’S FATE! Akiyama nearly took Kannon’s head off! A deafening pop goes out around the Gymnasium as Akiyama roars, seeing the limp form of the CEO at his feet. But he hasn’t had enough and grabs a handful of Xavier’s long red hair and yanks the dazed Scientologist to his feet, Kannon too groggy to do anything before Akiyama shove him down and sets him for a Straight Jacket Powerbomb to a huge roar of approval. Roaring once more, Akiyama effortlessly lifts Kannon up AN DRIVES HIS NECK AND SHOULDERS AS HARD AS GODLY POSSIBLE INTO THE MAT! Dave: MY GOD! I could FEEL that impact! Kannon is limp in Akiyama’s grip and he has no help forthcoming.. For once, Kannon is at someone’s MERCY! Keeping hold of the unconscious Scientologist, Akiyama lifts him right back up off the apron before holding him up as high as he can reach.... THEN DRILLING KANNON’S NECK AND SHOULDERS DOWN INTO THE MAT ONCE MORE!!! Dave: Something has to break here... It might be the ring or it might just be Xavier! A sick thud sounds out as Kannon’s bones drive into the ring but Akiyama is quick to jolt his body upright and pull Xavier back up one more time, parading him around for all in the Gymnasium to see that he HAS Kannon BEFORE WITH ALL HIS MIGHT DRIVING AND RELEASING XAVIER DOWN A THIRD TIME AND KANNON’S NECK BOUNCES OFF THE CANVAS AS HE FLIPS BACKWARDS!!! Dave: THE TRINITY BOMB! AKIYAMA MAY HAVE CRIPPLED XAVIER KANNON WITH THE MOST SICKENING TRINITY BOMB YOU WILL EVER SEE!!! The dazed and barely conscious bodies of Brandi and Gaz Green start to stir but only enough for them to make the foot long journey to the apron where they drop down to the floor, finally truly leaving Akiyama with Kannon. Dave: Xavier Kannon is in no state to defend himself here, this will only stop when Akiyama says so, and after all the cheap shots Xavier has given the NVG over the past few months... It may be some time! As Akiyama paces around the un-moving Kannon, the feed is suddenly lost and replaced with static before it comes back, grainy for a few seconds then returning to normal. Dave: I apologise for the technic-WAIT... I’m hearing something from Cal Seaver backstage! VENTING THEIR ANGER Cutting back we see that the RUSH workers have broken through the lines of security and are running riot backstage, destroying anything they see with the PCW logo. Huddling down in a corner is PCW reporter Cal Seaver, his eyes looking around nervously as he tries to report. Cal: Dave, the RUSH workers have gone berserk... Kannon had security waiting for them but they’ve been breached and the Shanghai Gymnasium is being destroyed by the inside out!!! In the background we see Marvin Harris taking a crowbar to TV Monitors, sending sparks flying as Mordred knocks another security member unconscious by ramming his head into the wall. To his left, Matt Elia yanks cables out of sockets while Matt Hunt throws a broken monitor through an outside window sending glass flying out into the night. Cal: Their rivalries have been forgotten, there are no sides.... Only RUSH and their an- Before Cal can finish, table debris flies over his head and his cameraman is knocked down, Joe Bananas picking the equipment up and pointing it at himself to shout something un-repeatable before hurling the camera into a wall. Dave: IT’S ALL FALLING APART, KANNON’S GRAND PLAN IS CRUMBLING ALL AROUND HIM! Returning to ringside, we catch Akiyama heaving the broken body of Xavier Kannon up once more, muttering some private words to him before spinning his limp form around to lock on a Full Nelson, again effortlessly pulling the CEO into the air AND EMBEDDING HIM INTO THE RING WITH THE HALCYON HAMMER!!! Thrusting his huge arms up into the air, the Shanghai crowd continue to cheer their lungs out for him before he again yanks Kannon up and grabs the back of his head, holding him up like a puppet. Dave: Xavier took some big bumps in his match with Duke, I don’t know how much more he can take at the hands of the giant Neo Void God! Running to the ropes, Akiyama hurls Kannon into the air AND SENDS THE CEO FLYING OVER THE TOP ROPES AND THROUGH THE AIR BEFORE LANDING IN A HEAP IN THE AISLE!!! Dave: As much as I never thought I’d say this.... I think Kannon has had enough! With his allies still not moving around ringside, Kannon lays motionless in the aisle as Akiyama vaults over the tope rope and lands beside him with grace. Fans lean as far as they can over the rails to try and touch the God as he picks up the Chair discarded by Brandi and tosses it down beside Kannon, pointing to the CEO then to the chair. Dave: .... And I think he’s about to end it! Utter focus in his eyes, Akiyama pulls Xavier up once more, holding the CEO’s face to his so he can stare right into the blurry eyes of Xavier, then, in one sweeping motion he whisks Kannon off his feet and onto his shoulder. The fans in the Shanghai Gymnasium know what is coming and cheer for it with all the air in their lungs. "A-D-A!!! A-D-A!!! A-D-A!!! A-D-A!!! A-D-A!!! A-D-A!!! A-D-A!!!" Then, swinging Kannon down off his shoulders, AKIYAMA SPIKES XAVIER’S NECK DOWN ONTO THE CHAIR WITH A ONE HANDED POWERBOMB TO COMPLETE THE ABSOLUTE DESTINY APOCALYPSE!!! "HOWY SHIT! HOWY SHIT! HOWY SHIT! HOWY SHIT! HOWY SHIT! HOWY SHIT!" Dave: THERE IT IS! THE ABSOLUTE DESTINY APOCALYPSE!!! KANNON IS.... DONE!!! Rearing his shoulders back, Akiyama’s muscled mass of a body ripples as his arms are thrust into the air, camera flashes still going off all around the Gymnasium before he looks down at Xavier. The Scientologist lays with his head resting on the chair, spread-eagled in the aisle and weakly breathing. With a content look in his eyes, Akiyama slowly lowers his hands, bringing the arena lights down with him. Dave: LADIES AND GENTLEMEN, AKIYAMA HAS RETURNED AND SINGLE-HANDEDLY DESTROYED THE FRONT OFFICE AND XAVIER KANNON! RUSH ARE NO MORE AND ARE WRECKING THE SHANGHAI GYMNASIUM AS I SPEAK IN COLLECTIVE RAGE! Only camera flashes now cut through the darkness, showing Xavier laying still in the aisle but with no sign of the Neo Void God beside him. Dave: XAVIER KANNON CAME INTO SHANGHAIED WITH A PLAN, AND FOR A BRIEF MOMENT IT LOOKED LIKE HE HAD PULLED IT OFF.... BUT ON THIS RARE OCCASION, IT ALL WENT WRONG FOR THE CEO AND HE’S SURE TO HAVE PAID FOR IT WITH BOTH HIS HEALTH AND HIS MONEY!!! FROM ME, DAVE KERN, GOODNIGHT AND BE SURE TO TUNE IN FOR WEDNESDAY TURMOIL... IF WE EVEN HAVE ONE AHEAD OF US!!! The PCW Copyright 2002 logo flashes up in the corner of the screen as we are shown backstage, split into four, where the RUSH workers continue their rampage. Stimpo and Ebola III armed with metal pipes smashing every single window of every car in the parking lot while Tyler Valentine kicks through the soundstage’s door and begins to smash up the equipment while the operators flee. Blayze is seen bursting into the Front Office’s Executive suite and smashing the glass table before striking match and dropping it onto the sofa before finally we cut to Marvin Harris staring at a wall that bears the PCW logo, picking up a chair and flinging it as hard as he can, smashing it through the wall and taking the logo with it as we fade to black on the destroyed PCW image.
